Build custom integrations, rate limits, approval flows, data connections, and more.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e🤖 \u003cstrong\u003eModels \u0026amp; Agents\u003c/strong\u003e: Wrap any base model with custom instructions, tools, and knowledge to build specialized agents. Supports dynamic variables, per-user/group access control, and community preset imports via \u003ca href=\"https://openwebui.com/\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eOpen WebUI Community\u003c/a\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e📝 \u003cstrong\u003eNotes\u003c/strong\u003e: A dedicated workspace for content outside conversations. Draft with a rich editor, use AI to rewrite selected text, and attach notes to any chat for full-context injection.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e📢 \u003cstrong\u003eChannels\u003c/strong\u003e: Real-time shared spaces where your team and AI models collaborate in one timeline. Tag models to draft or critique, with threads, reactions, pins, and access control.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e🧠 \u003cstrong\u003ePersistent Memory\u003c/strong\u003e: The AI remembers facts about you across conversations, carrying context from one chat to the next.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e✅ \u003cstrong\u003eLive Workflow \u0026amp; Message Flow\u003c/strong\u003e: Watch the AI build and work through checklists in real time. Queue messages while the AI is still responding; they send automatically when it's ready.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e📅 \u003cstrong\u003eCalendar \u0026amp; AI Scheduling\u003c/strong\u003e: Built-in personal and shared calendars with month/week/day views, recurring events, color coding, attendees, and reminders. Models manage your schedule conversationally through native function calling.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e⏱️ \u003cstrong\u003eAutomations\u003c/strong\u003e: Schedule prompts to run on recurring schedules, with runs surfaced on your calendar and each completed run linking back to the chat it produced.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e📱 \u003cstrong\u003eResponsive Design \u0026amp; PWA\u003c/strong\u003e: Seamless experience across desktop, laptop, and mobile, with a Progressive Web App for native app-like feel and offline access on localhost.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e✒️🔢 \u003cstrong\u003eFull Markdown and LaTeX Support\u003c/strong\u003e: Comprehensive Markdown and LaTeX capabilities for enriched interaction.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e🎤📹 \u003cstrong\u003eHands-Free Voice/Video Call\u003c/strong\u003e: Integrated voice and video calls with multiple Speech-to-Text providers (Local Whisper, OpenAI, Deepgram, Azure) and Text-to-Speech engines (Azure, ElevenLabs, OpenAI, Transformers, WebAPI).\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e💾 \u003cstrong\u003ePersistent Artifact Storage\u003c/strong\u003e: Built-in key-value storage API for artifacts, enabling journals, trackers, leaderboards, and collaborative tools with personal and shared data scopes.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e📚 \u003cstrong\u003eLocal RAG Integration\u003c/strong\u003e: Retrieval Augmented Generation backed by 9 vector databases and multiple content-extraction engines (Tika, Docling, Document Intelligence, Mistral OCR, PaddleOCR-vl, external loaders). Supports hybrid search (BM25 + vector) with reranking and full-context mode. Load documents into chat or pull them from your library with the \u003ccode\u003e#\u003c/code\u003e command.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e🔍 \u003cstrong\u003eWeb Search for RAG\u003c/strong\u003e: Search the web through dozens of providers including \u003ccode\u003eSearXNG\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eGoogle PSE\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eBrave Search\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eKagi\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eMojeek\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eTavily\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003ePerplexity\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eFirecrawl\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eserpstack\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eserper\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eSerply\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eDuckDuckGo\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eSearchApi\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eSerpApi\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eBing\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eJina\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eExa\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eSougou\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eAzure AI Search\u003c/code\u003e, and \u003ccode\u003eOllama Cloud\u003c/code\u003e, injecting results directly into the conversation.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e🌐 \u003cstrong\u003eWeb Browsing Capability\u003c/strong\u003e: Pull websites into chat with the \u003ccode\u003e#\u003c/code\u003e command followed by a URL, or let the model fetch them on its own when needed.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e🎨 \u003cstrong\u003eImage Generation \u0026amp; Editing\u003c/strong\u003e: Create and edit images with multiple engines including OpenAI DALL·E, Gemini, ComfyUI (local), and AUTOMATIC1111 (local), supporting both generation and prompt-based editing.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e⚙️ \u003cstrong\u003eMulti-Model Conversations\u003c/strong\u003e: Engage several models at once, harnessing their individual strengths in parallel for the best possible responses.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e📊 \u003cstrong\u003eUsage Analytics \u0026amp; Model Evaluation\u003c/strong\u003e: Admin dashboards track message volume, token consumption, and cost across users and models. Evaluate models with a built-in arena, A/B testing, and ELO-based leaderboards.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e🗄️ \u003cstrong\u003eFlexible Database \u0026amp; Storage\u003c/strong\u003e: Choose SQLite (with optional encryption) or PostgreSQL, and store files locally or on S3, Google Cloud Storage, or Azure Blob Storage.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e🧬 \u003cstrong\u003eAdvanced Vector Database Support\u003c/strong\u003e: Pick from 9 vector databases: ChromaDB, PGVector, Qdrant, Milvus, Elasticsearch, OpenSearch, Pinecone, S3Vector, and Oracle 23ai.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e🪪 \u003cstrong\u003eEnterprise Authentication \u0026amp; Provisioning\u003c/strong\u003e: Full LDAP/Active Directory integration, SSO via trusted headers and OAuth providers, and SCIM 2.0 automated provisioning for identity providers like Okta, Azure AD, and Google Workspace.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e☁️ \u003cstrong\u003eCloud-Native File Integration\u003c/strong\u003e: Native Google Drive and OneDrive/SharePoint file picking for seamless document import from enterprise cloud storage.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e🔭 \u003cstrong\u003eProduction Observability\u003c/strong\u003e: Built-in OpenTelemetry support for traces, metrics, and logs, plugging into your existing monitoring stack.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e⚖️ \u003cstrong\u003eHorizontal Scalability\u003c/strong\u003e: Redis-backed session management and WebSocket support for multi-worker, multi-node deployments behind load balancers.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e🌐🌍 \u003cstrong\u003eMultilingual Support\u003c/strong\u003e: Use Open WebUI in your preferred language with i18n support. We're actively seeking contributors to expand language coverage!\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e🌟 \u003cstrong\u003eContinuous Updates\u003c/strong\u003e: We're committed to improving Open WebUI with regular updates, fixes, and new features.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e🛡️ \u003cstrong\u003eTransparent Security Process\u003c/strong\u003e: Security reports are triaged, fixed, and published as open advisories through a documented responsible-disclosure process. Files, terminal, and git in a browser tab, reachable from your phone. Connect it into Open WebUI as a model, or reach it from Telegram, WhatsApp, and more.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e⚡ \u003cstrong\u003eOpen Terminal\u003c/strong\u003e and \u003cstrong\u003eTerminals (Enterprise)\u003c/strong\u003e (\u003ca href=\"https://github.com/open-webui/open-terminal\"\u003eopen-webui/open-terminal\u003c/a\u003e \u0026amp; \u003ca href=\"https://github.com/open-webui/terminals\"\u003eopen-webui/terminals\u003c/a\u003e): A self-hosted computing environment that plugs into Open WebUI, giving the AI a place to write code, run it, read output, fix errors, and iterate inside the chat. Terminals gives you per-user isolated containers with separate credentials, resource limits, and network rules. Automatic lifecycle management on Docker or Kubernetes.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e🔄 \u003cstrong\u003eoikb\u003c/strong\u003e (\u003ca href=\"https://github.com/open-webui/oikb\"\u003eopen-webui/oikb\u003c/a\u003e): Feed your Knowledge Bases from 45+ sources (GitHub, Confluence, ServiceNow, Salesforce, Jira, Slack, SharePoint, Notion, and more), keeping the tools your team already uses continuously in sync.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e🖥️ \u003cstrong\u003eNative Desktop App\u003c/strong\u003e (\u003ca href=\"https://github.com/open-webui/desktop\"\u003eopen-webui/desktop\u003c/a\u003e): Run Open WebUI as a native app on macOS, Windows, and Linux. System-wide Spotlight chat bar with screenshot capture, push-to-talk voice, and optional fully-local inference via a built-in llama.cpp engine.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eWant to learn more? Use the \u003ccode\u003e--network=host\u003c/code\u003e flag in your docker command to resolve this. Note that the port changes from 3000 to 8080, resulting in the link: \u003ccode\u003ehttp://localhost:8080\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eExample Docker class="markdown-body entry-content container-lg" itemprop="text"><div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h1 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Open WebUI 👋</h1><a id="user-content-open-webui-" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Open WebUI 👋" href="#open-webui-"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer nofollow" href="https://camo.githubusercontent.com/26427a0ed61c3edfe1b60d1f2bf2c3d7fd48dd46d9170843930b763469e45645/68747470733a2f2f696d672e736869656c64732e696f2f6769746875622f73746172732f6f70656e2d77656275692f6f70656e2d77656275693f7374796c653d736f6369616c"><img src="https://camo.githubusercontent.com/26427a0ed61c3edfe1b60d1f2bf2c3d7fd48dd46d9170843930b763469e45645/68747470733a2f2f696d672e736869656c64732e696f2f6769746875622f73746172732f6f70656e2d77656275692f6f70656e2d77656275693f7374796c653d736f6369616c" alt="GitHub stars" data-canonical-src="https://img.shields.io/github/stars/open-webui/open-webui?style=social" style="max-width: 100%;"></a>

<a href="https://github.com/sponsors/open-webui"><img src="https://camo.githubusercontent.com/d2432db86e1cfca636933728a664bf886cff501efab549a0af6abab26fe8851b/68747470733a2f2f696d672e736869656c64732e696f2f7374617469632f76313f6c6162656c3d53706f6e736f72266d6573736167653d254532253944254134266c6f676f3d47697448756226636f6c6f723d253233666538653836" alt="" data-canonical-src="https://img.shields.io/static/v1?label=Sponsor&amp;message=%E2%9D%A4&amp;logo=GitHub&amp;color=%23fe8e86" style="max-width: 100%;"></a></p>
<p dir="auto">Open WebUI is <strong>a home for AI</strong>, a self-hosted AI platform that's <strong><a href="https://docs.openwebui.com/features/extensibility/plugin/" rel="nofollow">extensible</a></strong>, <strong><a href="https://docs.openwebui.com/features/" rel="nofollow">feature-rich</a></strong>, user-friendly, and built to run <strong><a href="https://openwebui.com/sovereign-ai" rel="nofollow">entirely offline</a></strong>. With support for <strong>Ollama</strong> and <strong>OpenAI-compatible APIs</strong>, it gives you a powerful, provider-agnostic interface for both local and cloud-based models.</p>
<p dir="auto">Passionate about open-source AI? <a href="https://careers.openwebui.com/" rel="nofollow">Join our team →</a></p>
<p dir="auto"><a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" href="/open-webui/open-webui/blob/main/demo.png"><img src="/open-webui/open-webui/raw/main/demo.png" alt="Open WebUI Demo" style="max-width: 100%;"></a></p>
<div class="markdown-alert markdown-alert-tip" dir="auto"><p class="markdown-alert-title" dir="auto"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-light-bulb mr-2" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M8 1.5c-2.363 0-4 1.69-4 3.75 0 .984.424 1.625.984 2.304l.214.253c.223.264.47.556.673.848.284.411.537.896.621 1.49a.75.75 0 0 1-1.484.211c-.04-.282-.163-.547-.37-.847a8.456 8.456 0 0 0-.542-.68c-.084-.1-.173-.205-.268-.32C3.201 7.75 2.5 6.766 2.5 5.25 2.5 2.31 4.863 0 8 0s5.5 2.31 5.5 5.25c0 1.516-.701 2.5-1.328 3.259-.095.115-.184.22-.268.319-.207.245-.383.453-.541.681-.208.3-.33.565-.37.847a.751.751 0 0 1-1.485-.212c.084-.593.337-1.078.621-1.489.203-.292.45-.584.673-.848.075-.088.147-.173.213-.253.561-.679.985-1.32.985-2.304 0-2.06-1.637-3.75-4-3.75ZM5.75 12h4.5a.75.75 0 0 1 0 1.5h-4.5a.75.75 0 0 1 0-1.5ZM6 15.25a.75.75 0 0 1 .75-.75h2.5a.75.75 0 0 1 0 1.5h-2.5a.75.75 0 0 1-.75-.75Z"></path></svg>Tip</p><p dir="auto"><strong>Looking for an <a href="https://docs.openwebui.com/enterprise" rel="nofollow">Enterprise Plan</a>?</strong> – <strong><a href="https://docs.openwebui.com/enterprise" rel="nofollow">Speak with Our Sales Team Today!</a></strong></p>
</div>
<p dir="auto">For more information, be sure to check out our <a href="https://docs.openwebui.com/" rel="nofollow">Open WebUI Documentation</a>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Key Features of Open WebUI ⭐</h2><a id="user-content-key-features-of-open-webui-" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Key Features of Open WebUI ⭐" href="#key-features-of-open-webui-"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>
<p dir="auto">🚀 <strong>Effortless Setup</strong>: Install seamlessly via pip, uv, Docker, or Kubernetes (kubectl, kustomize, or helm), with <code>:ollama</code> and <code>:cuda</code> tagged images available for container deployments.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto">🤝 <strong>Broad Model &amp; API Integration</strong>: Connect any OpenAI-compatible API alongside local Ollama models. Point the API URL at <strong>LMStudio, GroqCloud, Mistral, OpenRouter, vLLM, and more</strong> to mix and match providers freely.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto">🔐 <strong>Granular RBAC &amp; User Groups</strong>: Administrators define detailed roles, groups, and permissions, giving each user exactly the access they need. Secure by default, with tailored experiences per group.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto">🧩 <strong>Plugin Support</strong>: Extend Open WebUI with <strong>Filters</strong>, <strong>Actions</strong>, <strong>Pipes</strong>, <strong>Tools</strong>, and <strong>Skills</strong>. Connect external services through <strong>MCP</strong>, <strong>MCPO</strong>, and <strong>OpenAPI tool servers</strong>. Build custom integrations, rate limits, approval flows, data connections, and more.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto">🤖 <strong>Models &amp; Agents</strong>: Wrap any base model with custom instructions, tools, and knowledge to build specialized agents. Supports dynamic variables, per-user/group access control, and community preset imports via <a href="https://openwebui.com/" rel="nofollow">Open WebUI Community</a>.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto">📝 <strong>Notes</strong>: A dedicated workspace for content outside conversations. Draft with a rich editor, use AI to rewrite selected text, and attach notes to any chat for full-context injection.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto">📢 <strong>Channels</strong>: Real-time shared spaces where your team and AI models collaborate in one timeline. Tag models to draft or critique, with threads, reactions, pins, and access control.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto">🧠 <strong>Persistent Memory</strong>: The AI remembers facts about you across conversations, carrying context from one chat to the next.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto">✅ <strong>Live Workflow &amp; Message Flow</strong>: Watch the AI build and work through checklists in real time. Queue messages while the AI is still responding; they send automatically when it's ready.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto">📅 <strong>Calendar &amp; AI Scheduling</strong>: Built-in personal and shared calendars with month/week/day views, recurring events, color coding, attendees, and reminders. Models manage your schedule conversationally through native function calling.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto">⏱️ <strong>Automations</strong>: Schedule prompts to run on recurring schedules, with runs surfaced on your calendar and each completed run linking back to the chat it produced.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto">📱 <strong>Responsive Design &amp; PWA</strong>: Seamless experience across desktop, laptop, and mobile, with a Progressive Web App for native app-like feel and offline access on localhost.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto">✒️🔢 <strong>Full Markdown and LaTeX Support</strong>: Comprehensive Markdown and LaTeX capabilities for enriched interaction.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto">🎤📹 <strong>Hands-Free Voice/Video Call</strong>: Integrated voice and video calls with multiple Speech-to-Text providers (Local Whisper, OpenAI, Deepgram, Azure) and Text-to-Speech engines (Azure, ElevenLabs, OpenAI, Transformers, WebAPI).</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto">💾 <strong>Persistent Artifact Storage</strong>: Built-in key-value storage API for artifacts, enabling journals, trackers, leaderboards, and collaborative tools with personal and shared data scopes.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto">📚 <strong>Local RAG Integration</strong>: Retrieval Augmented Generation backed by 9 vector databases and multiple content-extraction engines (Tika, Docling, Document Intelligence, Mistral OCR, PaddleOCR-vl, external loaders). Supports hybrid search (BM25 + vector) with reranking and full-context mode. Load documents into chat or pull them from your library with the <code>#</code> command.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto">🔍 <strong>Web Search for RAG</strong>: Search the web through dozens of providers including <code>SearXNG</code>, <code>Google PSE</code>, <code>Brave Search</code>, <code>Kagi</code>, <code>Mojeek</code>, <code>Tavily</code>, <code>Perplexity</code>, <code>Firecrawl</code>, <code>serpstack</code>, <code>serper</code>, <code>Serply</code>, <code>DuckDuckGo</code>, <code>SearchApi</code>, <code>SerpApi</code>, <code>Bing</code>, <code>Jina</code>, <code>Exa</code>, <code>Sougou</code>, <code>Azure AI Search</code>, and <code>Ollama Cloud</code>, injecting results directly into the conversation.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto">🌐 <strong>Web Browsing Capability</strong>: Pull websites into chat with the <code>#</code> command followed by a URL, or let the model fetch them on its own when needed.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto">🎨 <strong>Image Generation &amp; Editing</strong>: Create and edit images with multiple engines including OpenAI DALL·E, Gemini, ComfyUI (local), and AUTOMATIC1111 (local), supporting both generation and prompt-based editing.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto">⚙️ <strong>Multi-Model Conversations</strong>: Engage several models at once, harnessing their individual strengths in parallel for the best possible responses.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto">📊 <strong>Usage Analytics &amp; Model Evaluation</strong>: Admin dashboards track message volume, token consumption, and cost across users and models. Evaluate models with a built-in arena, A/B testing, and ELO-based leaderboards.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto">🗄️ <strong>Flexible Database &amp; Storage</strong>: Choose SQLite (with optional encryption) or PostgreSQL, and store files locally or on S3, Google Cloud Storage, or Azure Blob Storage.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto">🧬 <strong>Advanced Vector Database Support</strong>: Pick from 9 vector databases: ChromaDB, PGVector, Qdrant, Milvus, Elasticsearch, OpenSearch, Pinecone, S3Vector, and Oracle 23ai.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto">🪪 <strong>Enterprise Authentication &amp; Provisioning</strong>: Full LDAP/Active Directory integration, SSO via trusted headers and OAuth providers, and SCIM 2.0 automated provisioning for identity providers like Okta, Azure AD, and Google Workspace.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto">☁️ <strong>Cloud-Native File Integration</strong>: Native Google Drive and OneDrive/SharePoint file picking for seamless document import from enterprise cloud storage.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto">🔭 <strong>Production Observability</strong>: Built-in OpenTelemetry support for traces, metrics, and logs, plugging into your existing monitoring stack.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto">⚖️ <strong>Horizontal Scalability</strong>: Redis-backed session management and WebSocket support for multi-worker, multi-node deployments behind load balancers.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto">🌐🌍 <strong>Multilingual Support</strong>: Use Open WebUI in your preferred language with i18n support. We're actively seeking contributors to expand language coverage!</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto">🌟 <strong>Continuous Updates</strong>: We're committed to improving Open WebUI with regular updates, fixes, and new features.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto">🛡️ <strong>Transparent Security Process</strong>: Security reports are triaged, fixed, and published as open advisories through a documented responsible-disclosure process. See our <a href="https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/security">Security Policy</a>.</p>
</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">Want to learn more about Open WebUI's features? Check out our <a href="https://docs.openwebui.com/features" rel="nofollow">Open WebUI documentation</a> for a comprehensive overview!</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">The Open WebUI Ecosystem 🌐</h2><a id="user-content-the-open-webui-ecosystem-" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: The Open WebUI Ecosystem 🌐" href="#the-open-webui-ecosystem-"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Open WebUI is the core, surrounded by companion apps and infrastructure that extend what your AI can do, where it can reach, and how you run it:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>
<p dir="auto">💻 <strong>Open WebUI Computer</strong> (<a href="https://github.com/open-webui/computer">open-webui/computer</a>): A standalone, mobile-first computer and coding agent that runs on the machine you own. Files, terminal, and git in a browser tab, reachable from your phone. Connect it into Open WebUI as a model, or reach it from Telegram, WhatsApp, and more.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto">⚡ <strong>Open Terminal</strong> and <strong>Terminals (Enterprise)</strong> (<a href="https://github.com/open-webui/open-terminal">open-webui/open-terminal</a> &amp; <a href="https://github.com/open-webui/terminals">open-webui/terminals</a>): A self-hosted computing environment that plugs into Open WebUI, giving the AI a place to write code, run it, read output, fix errors, and iterate inside the chat. Terminals gives you per-user isolated containers with separate credentials, resource limits, and network rules. Automatic lifecycle management on Docker or Kubernetes.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto">🔄 <strong>oikb</strong> (<a href="https://github.com/open-webui/oikb">open-webui/oikb</a>): Feed your Knowledge Bases from 45+ sources (GitHub, Confluence, ServiceNow, Salesforce, Jira, Slack, SharePoint, Notion, and more), keeping the tools your team already uses continuously in sync.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto">🖥️ <strong>Native Desktop App</strong> (<a href="https://github.com/open-webui/desktop">open-webui/desktop</a>): Run Open WebUI as a native app on macOS, Windows, and Linux. System-wide Spotlight chat bar with screenshot capture, push-to-talk voice, and optional fully-local inference via a built-in llama.cpp engine.</p>
</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">Want to learn more? Check out our <a href="https://docs.openwebui.com" rel="nofollow">Open WebUI documentation</a> for more details!</p>
<hr>
<p dir="auto">We are incredibly grateful for the generous support of our sponsors. Their contributions help us to maintain and improve our project, ensuring we can continue to deliver quality work to our community. Thank you!</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">How to Install 🚀</h2><a id="user-content-how-to-install-" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: How to Install 🚀" href="#how-to-install-"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Installation via Python pip 🐍</h3><a id="user-content-installation-via-python-pip-" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Installation via Python pip 🐍" href="#installation-via-python-pip-"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Open WebUI can be installed using pip, the Python package installer. Before proceeding, ensure you're using <strong>Python 3.11</strong> to avoid compatibility issues.</p>
<ol dir="auto">
<li>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Install Open WebUI</strong>:
Open your terminal and run the following command to install Open WebUI:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="pip install open-webui"><pre>pip install open-webui</pre></div>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Running Open WebUI</strong>:
After installation, you can start Open WebUI by executing:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="open-webui serve"><pre>open-webui serve</pre></div>
</li>
</ol>
<p dir="auto">This will start the Open WebUI server, which you can access at <a href="http://localhost:8080" rel="nofollow">http://localhost:8080</a></p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Quick Start with Docker 🐳</h3><a id="user-content-quick-start-with-docker-" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Quick Start with Docker 🐳" href="#quick-start-with-docker-"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="markdown-alert markdown-alert-note" dir="auto"><p class="markdown-alert-title" dir="auto"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-info mr-2" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M0 8a8 8 0 1 1 16 0A8 8 0 0 1 0 8Zm8-6.5a6.5 6.5 0 1 0 0 13 6.5 6.5 0 0 0 0-13ZM6.5 7.75A.75.75 0 0 1 7.25 7h1a.75.75 0 0 1 .75.75v2.75h.25a.75.75 0 0 1 0 1.5h-2a.75.75 0 0 1 0-1.5h.25v-2h-.25a.75.75 0 0 1-.75-.75ZM8 6a1 1 0 1 1 0-2 1 1 0 0 1 0 2Z"></path></svg>Note</p><p dir="auto">Please note that for certain Docker environments, additional configurations might be needed. If you encounter any connection issues, our detailed guide on <a href="https://docs.openwebui.com/" rel="nofollow">Open WebUI Documentation</a> is ready to assist you.</p>
</div>
<div class="markdown-alert markdown-alert-warning" dir="auto"><p class="markdown-alert-title" dir="auto"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-alert mr-2" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M6.457 1.047c.659-1.234 2.427-1.234 3.086 0l6.082 11.378A1.75 1.75 0 0 1 14.082 15H1.918a1.75 1.75 0 0 1-1.543-2.575Zm1.763.707a.25.25 0 0 0-.44 0L1.698 13.132a.25.25 0 0 0 .22.368h12.164a.25.25 0 0 0 .22-.368Zm.53 3.996v2.5a.75.75 0 0 1-1.5 0v-2.5a.75.75 0 0 1 1.5 0ZM9 11a1 1 0 1 1-2 0 1 1 0 0 1 2 0Z"></path></svg>Warning</p><p dir="auto">When using Docker to install Open WebUI, make sure to include the <code>-v open-webui:/app/backend/data</code> in your Docker command. This step is crucial as it ensures your database is properly mounted and prevents any loss of data.</p>
</div>
<div class="markdown-alert markdown-alert-tip" dir="auto"><p class="markdown-alert-title" dir="auto"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-light-bulb mr-2" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M8 1.5c-2.363 0-4 1.69-4 3.75 0 .984.424 1.625.984 2.304l.214.253c.223.264.47.556.673.848.284.411.537.896.621 1.49a.75.75 0 0 1-1.484.211c-.04-.282-.163-.547-.37-.847a8.456 8.456 0 0 0-.542-.68c-.084-.1-.173-.205-.268-.32C3.201 7.75 2.5 6.766 2.5 5.25 2.5 2.31 4.863 0 8 0s5.5 2.31 5.5 5.25c0 1.516-.701 2.5-1.328 3.259-.095.115-.184.22-.268.319-.207.245-.383.453-.541.681-.208.3-.33.565-.37.847a.751.751 0 0 1-1.485-.212c.084-.593.337-1.078.621-1.489.203-.292.45-.584.673-.848.075-.088.147-.173.213-.253.561-.679.985-1.32.985-2.304 0-2.06-1.637-3.75-4-3.75ZM5.75 12h4.5a.75.75 0 0 1 0 1.5h-4.5a.75.75 0 0 1 0-1.5ZM6 15.25a.75.75 0 0 1 .75-.75h2.5a.75.75 0 0 1 0 1.5h-2.5a.75.75 0 0 1-.75-.75Z"></path></svg>Tip</p><p dir="auto">If you wish to utilize Open WebUI with Ollama included or CUDA acceleration, we recommend utilizing our official images tagged with either <code>:cuda</code> or <code>:ollama</code>. To enable CUDA, you must install the <a href="https://docs.nvidia.com/dgx/nvidia-container-runtime-upgrade/" rel="nofollow">Nvidia CUDA container toolkit</a> on your Linux/WSL system.</p>
</div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Installation with Default Configuration</h3><a id="user-content-installation-with-default-configuration" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Installation with Default Configuration" href="#installation-with-default-configuration"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>
<p dir="auto"><strong>If Ollama is on your computer</strong>, use this command:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="docker run -d -p 3000:8080 --add-host=host.docker.internal:host-gateway -v open-webui:/app/backend/data --name open-webui --restart always ghcr.io/open-webui/open-webui:main"><pre>docker run -d -p 3000:8080 --add-host=host.docker.internal:host-gateway -v open-webui:/app/backend/data --name open-webui --restart always ghcr.io/open-webui/open-webui:main</pre></div>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto"><strong>If Ollama is on a Different Server</strong>, use this command:</p>
<p dir="auto">To connect to Ollama on another server, change the <code>OLLAMA_BASE_URL</code> to the server's URL:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="docker run -d -p 3000:8080 -e OLLAMA_BASE_URL=https://example.com -v open-webui:/app/backend/data --name open-webui --restart always ghcr.io/open-webui/open-webui:main"><pre>docker run -d -p 3000:8080 -e OLLAMA_BASE_URL=https://example.com -v open-webui:/app/backend/data --name open-webui --restart always ghcr.io/open-webui/open-webui:main</pre></div>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto"><strong>To run Open WebUI with Nvidia GPU support</strong>, use this command:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="docker run -d -p 3000:8080 --gpus all --add-host=host.docker.internal:host-gateway -v open-webui:/app/backend/data --name open-webui --restart always ghcr.io/open-webui/open-webui:cuda"><pre>docker run -d -p 3000:8080 --gpus all --add-host=host.docker.internal:host-gateway -v open-webui:/app/backend/data --name open-webui --restart always ghcr.io/open-webui/open-webui:cuda</pre></div>
</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Installation for OpenAI API Usage Only</h3><a id="user-content-installation-for-openai-api-usage-only" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Installation for OpenAI API Usage Only" href="#installation-for-openai-api-usage-only"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>
<p dir="auto"><strong>If you're only using OpenAI API</strong>, use this command:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="docker run -d -p 3000:8080 -e OPENAI_API_KEY=your_secret_key -v open-webui:/app/backend/data --name open-webui --restart always ghcr.io/open-webui/open-webui:main"><pre>docker run -d -p 3000:8080 -e OPENAI_API_KEY=your_secret_key -v open-webui:/app/backend/data --name open-webui --restart always ghcr.io/open-webui/open-webui:main</pre></div>
</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Installing Open WebUI with Bundled Ollama Support</h3><a id="user-content-installing-open-webui-with-bundled-ollama-support" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Installing Open WebUI with Bundled Ollama Support" href="#installing-open-webui-with-bundled-ollama-support"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">This installation method uses a single container image that bundles Open WebUI with Ollama, allowing for a streamlined setup via a single command. Choose the appropriate command based on your hardware setup:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>
<p dir="auto"><strong>With GPU Support</strong>:
Utilize GPU resources by running the following command:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="docker run -d -p 3000:8080 --gpus=all -v ollama:/root/.ollama -v open-webui:/app/backend/data --name open-webui --restart always ghcr.io/open-webui/open-webui:ollama"><pre>docker run -d -p 3000:8080 --gpus=all -v ollama:/root/.ollama -v open-webui:/app/backend/data --name open-webui --restart always ghcr.io/open-webui/open-webui:ollama</pre></div>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto"><strong>For CPU Only</strong>:
If you're not using a GPU, use this command instead:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="docker run -d -p 3000:8080 -v ollama:/root/.ollama -v open-webui:/app/backend/data --name open-webui --restart always ghcr.io/open-webui/open-webui:ollama"><pre>docker run -d -p 3000:8080 -v ollama:/root/.ollama -v open-webui:/app/backend/data --name open-webui --restart always ghcr.io/open-webui/open-webui:ollama</pre></div>
</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">Both commands facilitate a built-in, hassle-free installation of both Open WebUI and Ollama, ensuring that you can get everything up and running swiftly.</p>
<p dir="auto">After installation, you can access Open WebUI at <a href="http://localhost:3000" rel="nofollow">http://localhost:3000</a>. Enjoy! 😄</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Other Installation Methods</h3><a id="user-content-other-installation-methods" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Other Installation Methods" href="#other-installation-methods"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">We offer various installation alternatives, including non-Docker native installation methods, Docker Compose, Kustomize, and Helm. Visit our <a href="https://docs.openwebui.com/getting-started/" rel="nofollow">Open WebUI Documentation</a> or join our <a href="https://discord.gg/5rJgQTnV4s" rel="nofollow">Discord community</a> for comprehensive guidance.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Troubleshooting</h3><a id="user-content-troubleshooting" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Troubleshooting" href="#troubleshooting"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

For further assistance and to join our vibrant community, visit the <a href="https://discord.gg/5rJgQTnV4s" rel="nofollow">Open WebUI Discord</a>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Open WebUI: Server Connection Error</h4><a id="user-content-open-webui-server-connection-error" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Open WebUI: Server Connection Error" href="#open-webui-server-connection-error"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">If you're experiencing connection issues, it’s often due to the WebUI docker container not being able to reach the Ollama server at 127.0.0.1:11434 (host.docker.internal:11434) inside the container . Use the <code>--network=host</code> flag in your docker command to resolve this. Note that the port changes from 3000 to 8080, resulting in the link: <code>http://localhost:8080</code>.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Example Docker Command</strong>:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="docker run -d --network=host -v open-webui:/app/backend/data -e OLLAMA_BASE_URL=http://127.0.0.1:11434 --name open-webui --restart always ghcr.io/open-webui/open-webui:main"><pre>docker run -d --network=host -v open-webui:/app/backend/data -e OLLAMA_BASE_URL=http://127.0.0.1:11434 --name open-webui --restart always ghcr.io/open-webui/open-webui:main</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Keeping Your Docker Installation Up-to-Date</h3><a id="user-content-keeping-your-docker-installation-up-to-date" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Keeping Your Docker Installation Up-to-Date" href="#keeping-your-docker-installation-up-to-date"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Check our Updating Guide available in our <a href="https://docs.openwebui.com/getting-started/updating" rel="nofollow">Open WebUI Documentation</a>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Using the Dev Branch 🌙</h3><a id="user-content-using-the-dev-branch-" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Using the Dev Branch 🌙" href="#using-the-dev-branch-"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="markdown-alert markdown-alert-warning" dir="auto"><p class="markdown-alert-title" dir="auto"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-alert mr-2" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M6.457 1.047c.659-1.234 2.427-1.234 3.086 0l6.082 11.378A1.75 1.75 0 0 1 14.082 15H1.918a1.75 1.75 0 0 1-1.543-2.575Zm1.763.707a.25.25 0 0 0-.44 0L1.698 13.132a.25.25 0 0 0 .22.368h12.164a.25.25 0 0 0 .22-.368Zm.53 3.996v2.5a.75.75 0 0 1-1.5 0v-2.5a.75.75 0 0 1 1.5 0ZM9 11a1 1 0 1 1-2 0 1 1 0 0 1 2 0Z"></path></svg>Warning</p><p dir="auto">The <code>:dev</code> branch contains the latest unstable features and changes. Use it at your own risk as it may have bugs or incomplete features.</p>
</div>
<p dir="auto">If you want to try out the latest bleeding-edge features and are okay with occasional instability, you can use the <code>:dev</code> tag like this:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="docker run -d -p 3000:8080 -v open-webui:/app/backend/data --name open-webui --add-host=host.docker.internal:host-gateway --restart always ghcr.io/open-webui/open-webui:dev"><pre>docker run -d -p 3000:8080 -v open-webui:/app/backend/data --name open-webui --add-host=host.docker.internal:host-gateway --restart always ghcr.io/open-webui/open-webui:dev</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Offline Mode</h3><a id="user-content-offline-mode" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Offline Mode" href="#offline-mode"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">If you are running Open WebUI in an offline environment, you can set the <code>HF_HUB_OFFLINE</code> environment variable to <code>1</code> to prevent attempts to download models from the internet.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="export HF_HUB_OFFLINE=1"><pre><span class="pl-k">export</span> HF_HUB_OFFLINE=1</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">What's Next? 🌟</h2><a id="user-content-whats-next-" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: What's Next? 🌟" href="#whats-next-"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
